43:T1080c,

import { BlockInfoCard } from "@/components/ui/block-info-card"

<BlockInfoCard type="quartr" color="#000000" />

Instructions d'utilisation [#instructions-dutilisation]

Integrate Quartr into the workflow. Look up public companies, corporate events, and event types; fetch AI-generated event summaries; list and download filings, reports, slide decks, and transcripts; and access archived audio and live event streams. Requires API Key.

Outils [#outils]

quartr_list_companies [#quartr_list_companies]

List companies covered by Quartr, filterable by ticker, ISIN, CIK, OpenFIGI, country, and exchange.

Entrée [#entrée]

| Paramètre       | Type   | Obligatoire | Description                                                                     |
| --------------- | ------ | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`        | string | Oui         | Quartr API key                                                                  |
| `tickers`       | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of company tickers (e.g., "AAPL,MSFT")                     |
| `isins`         | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of ISINs (e.g., "US0378331005")                            |
| `ciks`          | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of SEC CIKs (e.g., "0000320193")                           |
| `countries`     | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country codes (e.g., "US,SE")        |
| `exchanges`     | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of exchange symbols, without whitespace (e.g., "NasdaqGS") |
| `companyIds`    | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of Quartr company IDs (e.g., "4742,128")                   |
| `openfigis`     | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of OpenFIGI codes (figi, compositeFigi, or shareClassFigi) |
| `updatedAfter`  | string | Non         | Only return data updated after this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-01-01")          |
| `updatedBefore` | string | Non         | Only return data updated before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")         |
| `limit`         | number | Non         | Maximum number of items to return in a single request (default: 10, max: 500)   |
| `cursor`        | number | Non         | Pagination cursor from the previous response (nextCursor) for the next page     |
| `direction`     | string | Non         | Sort direction by id: "asc" or "desc" (default: asc)                            |

Sortie [#sortie]

| Paramètre       | Type   | Description                                                            |
| --------------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `companies`     | array  | Companies matching the filters                                         |
| ↳ `id`          | number | Quartr company ID                                                      |
| ↳ `name`        | string | Legal company name                                                     |
| ↳ `displayName` | string | Display name                                                           |
| ↳ `country`     | string | ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code                                        |
| ↳ `tickers`     | array  | Ticker listings for the company                                        |
| ↳ `ticker`      | string | Ticker symbol                                                          |
| ↳ `exchange`    | string | Exchange symbol                                                        |
| ↳ `isins`       | array  | ISINs for the company                                                  |
| ↳ `cik`         | string | SEC Central Index Key                                                  |
| ↳ `openfigi`    | array  | OpenFIGI share class identifiers                                       |
| ↳ `backlinkUrl` | string | Quartr backlink URL for the company                                    |
| ↳ `createdAt`   | string | Creation timestamp (ISO 8601)                                          |
| ↳ `updatedAt`   | string | Last update timestamp (ISO 8601)                                       |
| `nextCursor`    | number | Cursor for fetching the next page of results (null when no more pages) |

quartr_get_company [#quartr_get_company]

Retrieve a single company from Quartr by its company ID.

Entrée [#entrée-1]

| Paramètre   | Type   | Obligatoire | Description                    |
| ----------- | ------ | ----------- | ------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`    | string | Oui         | Quartr API key                 |
| `companyId` | number | Oui         | Quartr company ID (e.g., 4742) |

Sortie [#sortie-1]

| Paramètre       | Type   | Description                         |
| --------------- | ------ | ----------------------------------- |
| `company`       | object | The requested company               |
| ↳ `id`          | number | Quartr company ID                   |
| ↳ `name`        | string | Legal company name                  |
| ↳ `displayName` | string | Display name                        |
| ↳ `country`     | string | ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code     |
| ↳ `tickers`     | array  | Ticker listings for the company     |
| ↳ `ticker`      | string | Ticker symbol                       |
| ↳ `exchange`    | string | Exchange symbol                     |
| ↳ `isins`       | array  | ISINs for the company               |
| ↳ `cik`         | string | SEC Central Index Key               |
| ↳ `openfigi`    | array  | OpenFIGI share class identifiers    |
| ↳ `backlinkUrl` | string | Quartr backlink URL for the company |
| ↳ `createdAt`   | string | Creation timestamp (ISO 8601)       |
| ↳ `updatedAt`   | string | Last update timestamp (ISO 8601)    |

quartr_list_events [#quartr_list_events]

List corporate events (earnings calls, capital markets days, etc.) from Quartr, filterable by company, event type, and date range.

Entrée [#entrée-2]

| Paramètre       | Type   | Obligatoire | Description                                                                     |
| --------------- | ------ | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`        | string | Oui         | Quartr API key                                                                  |
| `companyIds`    | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of Quartr company IDs (e.g., "4742,128")                   |
| `tickers`       | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of company tickers (e.g., "AAPL,MSFT")                     |
| `isins`         | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of ISINs (e.g., "US0378331005")                            |
| `ciks`          | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of SEC CIKs (e.g., "0000320193")                           |
| `countries`     | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country codes (e.g., "US,SE")        |
| `exchanges`     | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of exchange symbols, without whitespace (e.g., "NasdaqGS") |
| `eventTypeIds`  | string | Non         | Comma-separated list of event type IDs (e.g., "26,27")                          |
| `startDate`     | string | Non         | Only return events on or after this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-01-01")          |
| `endDate`       | string | Non         | Only return events on or before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")         |
| `sortBy`        | string | Non         | Field to sort by: "id" or "date" (default: id)                                  |
| `updatedAfter`  | string | Non         | Only return data updated after this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-01-01")          |
| `updatedBefore` | string | Non         | Only return data updated before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")         |
| `limit`         | number | Non         | Maximum number of items to return in a single request (default: 10, max: 500)   |
| `cursor`        | number | Non         | Pagination cursor from the previous response (nextCursor) for the next page     |
| `direction`     | string | Non         | Sort direction applied to the sortBy field: "asc" or "desc" (default: asc)      |

Sortie [#sortie-2]

| Paramètre        | Type   | Description                                                            |
| ---------------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `events`         | array  | Events matching the filters                                            |
| ↳ `id`           | number | Quartr event ID                                                        |
| ↳ `companyId`    | number | Quartr company ID                                                      |
| ↳ `title`        | string | Event title (e.g., "Q1 2024")                                          |
| ↳ `date`         | string | Event date (ISO 8601)                                                  |
| ↳ `typeId`       | number | Event type ID                                                          |
| ↳ `fiscalYear`   | number | Fiscal year                                                            |
| ↳ `fiscalPeriod` | string | Fiscal period (e.g., "Q1")                                             |
| ↳ `language`     | string | Event language code                                                    |
| ↳ `backlinkUrl`  | string | Quartr backlink URL for the event                                      |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string | Creation timestamp (ISO 8601)                                          |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string | Last update timestamp (ISO 8601)                                       |
| `nextCursor`     | number | Cursor for fetching the next page of results (null when no more pages) |

quartr_get_event [#quartr_get_event]

Retrieve a single corporate event from Quartr by its event ID.

Entrée [#entrée-3]

| Paramètre | Type   | Obligatoire | Description                    |
| --------- | ------ | ----------- | ------------------------------ |
| `apiKey`  | string | Oui         | Quartr API key                 |
| `eventId` | number | Oui         | Quartr event ID (e.g., 128301) |

Sortie [#sortie-3]

| Paramètre        | Type   | Description                       |
| ---------------- | ------ | --------------------------------- |
| `event`          | object | The requested event               |
| ↳ `id`           | number | Quartr event ID                   |
| ↳ `companyId`    | number | Quartr company ID                 |
| ↳ `title`        | string | Event title (e.g., "Q1 2024")     |
| ↳ `date`         | string | Event date (ISO 8601)             |
| ↳ `typeId`       | number | Event type ID                     |
| ↳ `fiscalYear`   | number | Fiscal year                       |
| ↳ `fiscalPeriod` | string | Fiscal period (e.g., "Q1")        |
| ↳ `language`     | string | Event language code               |
| ↳ `backlinkUrl`  | string | Quartr backlink URL for the event |
| ↳ `createdAt`    | string | Creation timestamp (ISO 8601)     |
| ↳ `updatedAt`    | string | Last update timestamp (ISO 8601)  |

quartr_get_event_summary [#quartr_get_event_summary]

Retrieve the AI-generated summary of a corporate event from Quartr, with selectable length and optional plain-text formatting.

Entrée [#entrée-4]

| Paramètre       | Type    | Obligatoire | Description                                                                |
| --------------- | ------- | ----------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apiKey`        | string  | Oui         | Quartr API key                                                             |
| `eventId`       | number  | Oui         | Quartr event ID (e.g., 128301)                                             |
| `summaryLength` | string  | Non         | Length preset for the summary: "line", "short", or "long" (default: short) |
| `plainSummary`  | boolean | Non         | Return a plain-text summary without embedded document source tags          |

Sortie [#sortie-4]

| Paramètre          | Type   | Description                                                                                                              |
| ------------------ | ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `summary`          | string | AI-generated event summary in Markdown (includes embedded document source tags unless a plain-text summary is requested) |
| `sources`          | array  | Source documents referenced by the summary                                                                               |
| ↳ `sourceId`       | string | ID linking the source document to tags embedded in the summary                                                           |
| ↳ `documentId`     | number | Quartr document ID of the source                                                                                         |
| ↳ `page`           | number | Page number or timestamp in seconds depending on the document type                                                       |
| ↳ `timestamp`      | number | Timestamp in seconds                                                                                                     |
| ↳ `typeId`         | number | Document type ID of the source                                                                                           |
| `summaryId`        | number | Quartr summary ID                                                                                                        |
| `summaryCreatedAt` | string | Summary creation timestamp (ISO 8601)                                                                                    |
| `summaryUpdatedAt` | string | Summary last update timestamp (ISO 8601)                                                                                 |

| `endDate`          | string  | Non         | Only return documents dated on or before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")                                                                                            |
| `expandEvent`      | boolean | Non         | Include expanded event details on each document                                                                                                                             |

| `updatedBefore`    | string  | Non         | Only return data updated before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")                                                                                                     |

| `endDate`       | string  | Non         | Only return audio dated on or before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")    |
| `expandEvent`   | boolean | Non         | Include expanded event details on each audio recording                          |
| `updatedAfter`  | string  | Non         | Only return data updated after this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-01-01")          |
| `updatedBefore` | string  | Non         | Only return data updated before this ISO 8601 date (e.g., "2024-12-31")         |
| `limit`         | number  | Non         | Maximum number of items to return in a single request (default: 10, max: 500)   |
| `cursor`        | number  | Non         | Pagination cursor from the previous response (nextCursor) for the next page     |
| `direction`     | string  | Non         | Sort direction by id: "asc" or "desc" (default: asc)                            |
